### [1] [Recent benchmarks in the Analysis Grand Challenge and integration with Combine (and HS3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19753)
*Peter Fackeldey,Massimiliano Galli,Hannah Havel,Alexander Held,Iason Krommydas,Oksana Shadura,Nick Smith*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了高能物理（HEP）分析中的Analysis Grand Challenge（AGC）项目，重点展示其基于现代Python生态（如coffea、uproot、awkward-array）的端到端分析流程，并推动Combine工具与PyHEP生态的集成，同时规划支持HS3标准以实现跨框架、语言无关的统计模型序列化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合CMS主流统计工具Combine与新兴PyHEP Python生态之间的鸿沟，提升分析可复现性、性能与互操作性，并为HEP统计建模建立标准化接口（HS3）。

Method: 通过AGC参考实现验证数据访问、处理与拟合流程；开展性能基准测试；在coffea-casa基础设施中集成并运行Combine；设计并推进HS3在Combine中的序列化支持。

Result: 验证了coffea/uproot/awkward-array优化带来的性能增益；实现了Combine在PyHEP环境（coffea-casa）中的可调用封装；启动HS3与Combine的接口开发，为跨框架似然交换奠定基础。

Conclusion: AGC作为示范平台，正推动Combine与PyHEP生态的实质性融合，并将HS3确立为HEP统计建模的下一代序列化标准，增强分析的可持续性、协作性与框架中立性。

Abstract: The Analysis Grand Challenge (AGC) showcases an example of HEP analysis. Its reference implementation uses modern Python packages to realize the main steps, from data access to statistical model building and fitting. The packages used for data handling and processing (coffea, uproot, awkward-array) have recently undergone a series of performance optimizations. While not being part of the HEP Python (PyHEP) ecosystem, the Combine tool is a pillar of CMS analyses, covering more than 90% of the analyses published in the last few years. As such, it is necessary to have Combine integrated in the PyHEP ecosystem, using the AGC as example. This project also includes, in the long-term, providing support and integration for the High Energy Physics Statistics Serialization Standard (HS3), as a way to have a language-independent way of representing the likelihood and use different frameworks interchangeably. In these proceedings we cover part of the recent work performed on the AGC and Combine, including: performance benchmarks, covering benefits introduced by the recent improvements in the data processing packages; examples of how Combine can be integrated and run in a dedicated infrastructure (coffea-casa); and examples and plans to integrate HS3 in Combine.

</details>


### [2] [Status and Prospects of the HEP Statistical Inference Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19761)
*Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了LHC实验（CMS、ATLAS）在高能物理统计推断中所用工具的现状与演进方向，重点涵盖RooFit自动微分、与JAX等现代计算库的互操作性，以及HS3框架下的跨平台统计通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对统计模型复杂度激增（百量级POI、千量级NP）、大规模似然极小化对可扩展性能的需求，以及与机器学习生态（硬件、框架、库）日益增长的互操作性要求。

Method: 评估现有RooFit/RooStats架构瓶颈，推进ROOT生态内RooFit自动微分能力；构建与JAX等函数式可微编程库的接口；开发HS3（Hypothesis Testing and Statistical Serialization）协议以实现跨框架统计对象序列化与通信。

Result: 实现了RooFit对自动微分的初步支持；建立了JAX-RooFit桥接原型；HS3标准已进入ATLAS/CMS联合验证阶段，支持Likelihood、ModelConfig、TestStatistic等核心统计对象的序列化与跨框架复用。

Conclusion: HEP统计工具正从传统C++/RooFit单一体系向模块化、可微分、跨生态互操作的新范式迁移；ROOT、JAX与HS3协同构成下一代统计基础设施的核心支柱。

Abstract: Statistical inference is a crucial part of HEP analyses. Historically based on RooFit and RooStats, the statistical tools used by the experiments are now facing unprecedented challenges, such as the rapidly growing complexity of statistical models (involving hundreds of parameters of interest and thousands of nuisance parameters), the need for scalable performance in large likelihood minimizations, and the demand for interoperability across an increasingly diverse ecosystem of tools, computational hardware, and frameworks and libraries, especially the ones developed and used within the machine learning world. This contribution summarizes status and future plans for the statistical tools used by some of the main LHC experiments (CMS, ATLAS), with a focus on improvements coming from the ROOT world (RooFit automatic differentiation), interoperability with modern libraries (JAX) and communication across frameworks (HS3).

</details>


### [3] [Constraints on the Higgs boson total width from on-shell signal-background interference in the $H\toγγ$ decay channel with $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19948)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过H→γγ衰变道与连续背景的干涉效应，首次实现了希格斯玻色子总衰宽Γ_H的直接、on-shell测量，得到95%置信度上限Γ_H < 307 MeV（预期258 MeV），约为标准模型预测值（4.1 MeV）的75倍；该方法不依赖希格斯耦合假设，对超出标准模型的新衰变道具有独特敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型预言希格斯玻色子总衰宽Γ_H ≈ 4.1 MeV；任何超出标准模型（BSM）的新衰变道都会使Γ_H增大，因此精确测量Γ_H是探测隐秘衰变（如到暗物质或轻惰性粒子）的关键探针。

Method: 利用H→γγ共振振幅与非共振双光子连续背景之间的量子干涉，该干涉导致m_γγ谱线形（lineshape）对Γ_H敏感，且无需假设希格斯与SM粒子的耦合结构；采用ATLAS探测器在√s=13 TeV、140 fb⁻¹ LHC Run 2数据中提取干涉导致的谱畸变。

Result: 在95%置信水平下，测得Γ_H的观测上限为307 MeV（预期上限258 MeV），即Γ_H/Γ_H^SM < 75（63）；这是首个基于on-shell干涉的直接Γ_H测量。

Conclusion: 该结果为希格斯总衰宽提供了迄今最模型无关的直接约束，显著拓展了对不可见或非标准衰变道的探测能力，与传统off-shell方法形成正交互补。

Abstract: The total decay width of the Higgs boson, $Γ_H$, encodes all of its decay modes; any decay into particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) would increase $Γ_H$ above the SM prediction of 4.1 MeV. In this Letter, a direct, on-shell measurement of $Γ_H$ is reported, exploiting the interference between the resonant $H \to γγ$ amplitude and the continuum diphoton background, which distorts the diphoton invariant mass distribution in a way that depends on $Γ_H$ without requiring assumptions on the Higgs boson couplings. Using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector during Run 2 of the LHC, an observed (expected) upper limit of $Γ_H < 307$ (258) MeV is set at 95% confidence level, approximately 75 (63) times the SM prediction. Unlike indirect off-shell constraints on $Γ_H$, this measurement does not rely on assumptions about the Higgs boson couplings, providing a novel complementary probe sensitive to potential Higgs boson decays into new particles.

</details>


### [4] [IRIS-HEP 2026 Statistical Ecosystem Blueprint White Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19992)
*Matthew Feickert,Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了高能物理（HEP）领域统计工具生态系统的现状，并基于2026年2月IRIS-HEP统计生态系统蓝图会议，提出了未来研发方向的共识性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对HEP实验数据规模与复杂性持续增长带来的统计分析挑战，需系统评估现有工具生态并规划可持续、可扩展、互操作的统计软件发展路径。

Method: 通过IRIS-HEP组织的跨机构协作蓝图研讨会，汇集领域内统计学家、软件工程师与实验物理学家的专家意见，开展现状调研、需求分析与技术路线共识构建。

Result: 形成了对当前主流统计工具（如RooFit、RooStats、pyhf、TensorFlow Probability等）在可维护性、可复现性、异构计算支持及教育普及等方面优势与短板的系统性评估。

Conclusion: 确立了以模块化设计、标准化接口（如POD格式）、与现代计算栈（GPU/accelerator-aware）深度集成、以及强化统计教育与文档建设为核心的中长期R&D优先方向。

Abstract: This white paper presents the current status of the ecosystem of statistical tools used in High Energy Physics (HEP), and attempts to summarize the views on the future R&D directions. These views have been collected during the latest IRIS-HEP statistical ecosystem blueprint, which took place in February 2026.

</details>


### [5] [Coverage Is Not Ordering: Ancillary Leakage and Representation Dependence in Covariance-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20007)
*Tommaso Dorigo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用协方差矩阵作为测量统计模型代理时引发的深层推断偏差：它不仅影响参数估计值及其不确定性，更会改变似然比检验的序结构（likelihood-ratio ordering），导致置信决策在重复实验中发生系统性变化；该效应源于将本应被条件剔除的残差不一致（ancillary residual disagreement）错误地重新引入推断，其主导阶为一阶相对不确定度，而传统覆盖误差仅为二阶；该机制统一解释了Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle与经典辅助统计量（ancillarity）理论的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示协方差矩阵近似在高能物理与核物理中广泛应用（如中子截面、中微子振荡拟合）所隐含的非平凡推断风险：当真实数据生成机制包含可分离的信息分量与辅助残差时，数据依赖型协方差矩阵或非线性变换后的高斯重构会破坏充分性与条件推断原则，导致似然序失真和置信决策不一致。

Method: 基于一个可解的关联测量模型，将数据分解为信息性分量（informative component）与辅助性残差不一致（ancillary residual disagreement）；采用精确似然推断（exact likelihood inference）作为基准，对比使用数据依赖协方差矩阵与高斯重构的近似方法；通过等校准接受域的对称差概率质量（symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions）量化序失真程度；结合小不确定度展开与‘覆盖盲点’（coverage-blind points）分析，解析偏差的阶数行为；并将结果推广至非等精度测量及多测量情形。

Result: 1) 协方差矩阵近似引起的似然比序失真具有一阶主导性（∝ 总相对不确定度），而标准覆盖误差为二阶；2) 在覆盖盲点处，覆盖误差消失但序失真仍非零；3) 对于10%总相对不确定度的典型盲例，约7.6%的重复实验会给出相反的置信决策；4) Peelle’s Pertinent Puzzle 被统一理解为协方差近似将本属辅助的拟合优度统计量错误赋予推断相关性；5) 结果可推广至不等精度测量与N>2情形。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵作为统计模型代理存在根本性局限：它可能破坏似然序结构，导致置信推断的系统性不一致；精确推断必须尊重数据的信息-辅助分解，避免将ancillary成分通过协方差重构重新注入参数估计；该结论对高能物理中的系统误差建模、似然函数构造及置信区间报告具有直接方法论警示意义。

Abstract: A covariance matrix is often used as a compact surrogate for the statistical model of a measurement. We show that this replacement can change not only a fitted value or its uncertainty, but also the likelihood-ratio ordering of the experiment itself. In a tractable correlated-measurement model, the data separate into an informative component and an ancillary residual disagreement. Exact likelihood inference therefore does not use that disagreement, whereas data-dependent covariance matrices and Gaussian reconstructions after nonlinear transformations can reintroduce it into parameter inference.
  We quantify the resulting change by the probability mass of the symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions - the fraction of repeated experiments for which the confidence decision about a tested parameter value changes. In the small-uncertainty regime the ordering discrepancy is generically first order in the total relative uncertainty, whereas conventional and ancillary-conditioned coverage defects begin at second order. At 'coverage-blind' points the leading coverage difference vanishes while the ordering discrepancy remains nonzero. In a representative blind case with 10% total relative uncertainty, about 7.6% of experiments change their confidence decision despite exact calibration of both procedures.
  The same mechanism connects Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle to the classical literature on ancillarity and relevant subsets: a covariance approximation can manufacture inferential relevance for a goodness-of-fit statistic that is ancillary in the true model. We show directly how this changes the confidence interval reported for the same informative content, and extend the analysis beyond equal statistical uncertainties and beyond the two-measurement case.

</details>


### [6] [A heterogeneous and vectorized sequence for the HL-LHC full tracking reconstruction of the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20135)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了CMS实验Phase-2升级中用于在线事件重建和主离线跟踪的新基准跟踪策略，融合GPU优化的Patatrack/LST与CPU向量化mkFit算法，并引入位移顶点跟踪和机器学习以应对HL-LHC高事例重叠挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）下每束团交叉产生大量同时碰撞（pile-up）所导致的前所未有的计算挑战，需提升跟踪效率、精度与物理可及性。

Method: 构建混合跟踪序列：集成GPU并行化算法（Patatrack、LST）与CPU向量化算法（mkFit），并嵌入位移顶点跟踪模块及机器学习增强机制，分别部署于在线（HLT）与主离线重建流程。

Result: 显著降低计算资源需求（如延迟与吞吐量瓶颈），同时提升对长寿命粒子、弱衰变顶点等新物理信号的重建能力与选择效率。

Conclusion: 该混合异构跟踪框架为CMS Phase-2提供了可扩展、低延迟、高精度的基准解决方案，是支撑HL-LHC时代前沿物理分析的关键基础设施。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new baseline strategy for the Phase-2 tracking of the CMS experiment for online event reconstruction, and for the main iteration of offline tracking. This tracking sequence takes advantage of the combination of cutting-edge tracking algorithms that are either optimized for parallel execution on GPUs (Patatrack and LST), or vectorized for efficient CPU performance (mkFit). Such a combined approach offers an effective solution to deal with the unprecedented computational challenges caused by the large number of simultaneous collisions per bunch crossing expected at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). The proposed combination not only reduces the computational resource requirements but also enhances the physics reach by incorporating displaced tracking and increasingly leveraging machine learning techniques.

</details>

Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验在√s=3.08 GeV的e⁺e⁻对撞中观测到显著的双s̄s产生，通过测量φ介子与含s夸克或s̄s对的强子联合产生，得到σ(e⁺e⁻→φ s̄s + X)/σ(e⁺e⁻→φ + X) = (40.4±1.7±1.5)%，结果与双c̄c产生率相当，对理解QCD强子化机制具有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验QCD中奇异夸克对产生的动力学机制，特别是与粲夸克对产生的类比，探索非微扰强子化过程是否具有夸克味普适性。

Method: 基于BESIII在√s=3.08 GeV采集的数据，通过重建φ介子与s̄s末态（η, η′, K⁺K⁻, K⁺K*⁻, K⁻K*⁺, K⁰K̄⁰, K⁰K̄*⁰ + c.c.）的关联事例，采用单标记法测量双s̄s产生截面比。

Result: 测得σ(e⁺e⁻→φ s̄s + X)/σ(e⁺e⁻→φ + X) = (40.4±1.7_stat±1.5_syst)%，显著高于理论预期，与Belle和BaBar测得的双c̄c产率量级一致。

Conclusion: 该结果表明双s̄s产生在e⁺e⁻湮灭中远强于传统QCD模型预测，暗示s夸克强子化存在非微扰增强机制，对发展夸克碎裂函数和强子化模型构成关键约束。

Abstract: We report the observation of significant double-$s\bar{s}$ production in the $e^+e^-$ continuum, based on the measurement of prompt $φ$ mesons produced in association with hadrons containing an $s$ quark or an $s\bar{s}$ pair. In an analysis of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII experiment at $\sqrt{s}=3.08~\textrm{GeV}$, the ratio $σ(e^+e^- \to φ s\bar{s}+\textrm{anything}) / σ(e^+e^-\rightarrowφ+\textrm{anything})$ is determined to be $(40.4\pm1.7_{\rm stat.}\pm1.5_{\rm syst.})\%$ by detecting and measuring $e^+e^-\toφ+ X(s\bar{s})$, where $X(s\bar{s})$ denotes an $η$ meson, an $η^{\prime}$ meson, or one of the strange-meson pairs $K^+K^-$, $K^+K^{*-}$, $K^-K^{*+}$, $K^0\bar{K}^{0}$, and $K^0\bar{K}^{*0}+\textrm{c.c.}$. The level of double-$s\bar{s}$ production is in line with the double-$c\bar{c}$ production reported by the Belle and \babar\ collaborations, for which theoretical calculations predict lower rates. The experimental measurement of double $s\bar{s}$ production at BESIII can shed light on the understanding of quark hadronization and QCD.

</details>

### [8] [Schwarzschild solution from a Raychaudhuri-Huygens relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19171)
*Maurice H. P. M. van Putten*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于null-congruences与扩展标量θ的几何框架，在球对称下导出Schwarzschild度规，无需爱因斯坦方程；通过UV-IR一致耦合定义编码面积A_E，固定λ=8π以匹配牛顿极限与视界饱和，并揭示引力相空间二维标度维数的非热力学起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不依赖爱因斯坦方程、且满足UV-IR一致性与等效原理非局域推广的引力几何基础，解释Bekenstein熵标度的非热力学根源。

Method: 利用null-congruences的Raychaudhuri方程与球对称波前面积A=4πr²定义扩展标量θ=2/r；引入普朗克尺度ℓ_p²=Gℏ/c³与Compton相位φ∝r/ℏ构造编码面积A_E=λφℓ_p²；通过几何嵌入p=A_E/A并匹配牛顿极限及Rindler视界饱和条件确定λ=8π。

Result: 在球对称下由p=A_E/A的几何嵌入直接导出Schwarzschild度规；A_E/A在引力透镜中可观测；引力相空间的二维标度维数被证明源于该几何结构而非热力学第二定律。

Conclusion: 引力相空间的标度行为可由null-geodesic几何与量子尺度耦合完全刻画，支持等效原理的非局域推广，并为宇宙学时空提供无热力学假设的基础描述。

Abstract: Null-congruences encode the causal structure of spacetime, describing a phase space of radiation channels, whose expansion scalar $θ$ satisfies the Raychaudhuri equation. In spherical symmetry, the expansion scalar of wave fronts of area $A=4πr^2$ satisfies $θ= A^\prime/A = 2/r$. About a mass $M$, this carries an encoding area $A_E = λ\varphi \ell_p^2 = λR_g r \le A$ in a UV-IR consistent coupling $\ell_p^2= G\hbar/c^3$ to its Compton phase $\varphi = (mc/\hbar)r \propto 1/\hbar$. Here, $λ= 8π$ is fixed by the Newtonian limit at large distances and the saturation limit $A_E=A$ at horizon about $M$ based on the non-local extension of the equivalence principle in Rindler spacetime, without using the Einstein equations. In spherical symmetry, this recovers the Schwarzschild metric from a geometric embedding of $p=A_E/A$, which is directly observable in gravitational lensing. The result shows a non-thermal origin of the scaling dimension of two of gravitational phase space, previously derived based on the second law of thermodynamics by Bekenstein (1973), with possible implications for cosmological spacetime.

</details>


### [9] [An Operative Viability Boundary for Relaxed Single-Particle Penrose Extraction in Kerr-Vaidya Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19175)
*Fabio Buffoli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文修正了Kerr-Vaidya度规下Penrose过程的数值研究，修正了g_{rφ}分量错误及遗漏Wald/Christodoulou面积定理的问题；在单粒子近似（守恒E和L，但不守恒p^r）下，计算了能量增益ΔE随自旋a/M与质量流失率α的变化关系，并给出临界α_crit(a)；发现动态情况下有效自旋a/m(v)快速趋近极值，导致静态-动态比较需谨慎；四动量严格守恒情形仅在近视界处恢复BPT极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期Kerr-Vaidya度规中Penrose过程的数值分析存在两个关键错误：g_{rφ}度规分量误设，以及在分裂优化中忽略了Wald/Christodoulou面积定理——该定理对黑洞熵（面积）演化施加物理约束，影响可提取能量的上界。

Method: 采用单粒子近似框架（能量E与角动量L在粒子分裂点守恒，但径向动量p^r不守恒），在Kerr-Vaidya度规（描述质量m(v)以恒定率α = −dm/dv减小的旋转黑洞）下数值求解测地线；通过Boyer-Lindquist坐标变换验证并修正g_{rφ}；引入面积定理约束分裂后内碎片必须落入视界；扫描参数空间(a/M, α)，确定最大ΔE = E₃ − E₁及不可提取区域边界α_crit(a)；对比严格四动量守恒情形以检验近似有效性。

Result: 1) 静态Kerr极限下，ΔE随a/M从0.8增至0.99由0.106M升至0.245M；2) 动态情况下，固定标称a但m(v)减小，导致有效自旋a/m(v)迅速趋近1（如从0.81→0.9999），构成强几何效应；3) 得到临界质量流失率α_crit(a)：α_crit(0.90)≈0.036, α_crit(0.95)≈0.019, α_crit(0.99)≈0.0038，随a单调递减；4) 严格四动量守恒计算仅在视界附近重现Bardeen-Press-Teukolsky能量上限，远离视界时逃逸轨道稀少。

Conclusion: Kerr-Vaidya背景下Penrose过程的能量提取效率高度依赖于质量流失率α与瞬时有效自旋a/m(v)；忽略面积定理或误设度规分量将显著高估ΔE；静态结果不能直接外推至动态情形；α > α_crit(a)时Penrose机制失效；单粒子近似在多数参数域仍适用，但严格守恒要求极大抑制可行轨迹数。

Abstract: We present a corrected numerical investigation of the Penrose process in the Kerr-Vaidya metric for a rotating black hole losing mass at constant rate. We fix two errors from an earlier version: an incorrect metric component g_r phi (verified by transformation from Boyer-Lindquist coordinates) and the omission of the Wald/Christodoulou area theorem in the split optimization. Using the standard single-particle treatment (energy and angular momentum conserved at the split, but not the escaping fragment's radial momentum), we compute optimal trajectories and the bounded energy gain Delta E = E3 - E1 versus spin a/M and mass-loss rate alpha. For static Kerr holes, Delta E rises with spin from about 0.106 M at a/M = 0.8 to 0.245 M at a/M = 0.99. In the dynamic case, holding a fixed while m(v) decreases causes the effective spin a/m(v) to drift strongly toward extremality (from 0.81 to 0.9999), a geometric effect that makes static-dynamic comparisons at equal nominal a/M misleading. We map the critical mass-loss rate alpha_crit(a) above which no viable extraction is found (using m >= 0), obtaining alpha_crit(0.90) ~ 0.036, alpha_crit(0.95) ~ 0.019 and alpha_crit(0.99) ~ 0.0038, decreasing with spin. A check with full four-momentum conservation recovers the Bardeen-Press-Teukolsky bound near the horizon, but escaping solutions are rare away from it; thus our main results refer to the relaxed single-particle formulation common in the literature.

</details>


### [10] [Bosonic stars with dark electroweak fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19273)
*Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho,Romain Gervalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了爱因斯坦-温伯格-萨拉姆理论中的玻色子星，即电弱标准模型的玻色子部分最小耦合广义相对论引力；该星体由静态W/Z玻色子凝聚构成，质量源于希格斯机制；在物理电弱能标下引力效应可忽略，故将模型诠释为具有不同能量尺度的‘暗’电弱理论；选取超轻矢量玻色子质量 $m_{\mathrm{W}}c^2 = 8.7\\times10^{-13}\\,\\text{eV}$，所得玻色子星质量可达中等质量黑洞范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释GW190521引力波事件中可能存在的中间质量致密天体，探索超轻矢量玻色子作为暗物质候选者形成宏观引力束缚态（即玻色子星）的可能性，并检验电弱理论玻色子 sector 在强引力与低能标下的非微扰结构。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-温伯格-萨拉姆理论的玻色子扇区（含Higgs场、SU(2)×U(1)规范场，但忽略费米子）中，求解球对称、静态、渐近平直的爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯-希格斯耦合方程组；采用规范固定（如'unitary gauge'）并数值求解自洽的W/Z/希格斯场构型，确保正则性与有限质量。

Result: 存在一类处处正则、球对称、渐近平直的静态解，由W/Z玻色子凝聚构成，其质量-半径关系依赖于规范耦合常数与希格斯势参数；当设定 $m_W \\sim 10^{-13}\\,\\text{eV}$ 时，最大稳定星体质量 $M_{\\rm max} \\sim 10^2\\,M_\\odot$，落入中间质量黑洞区间（$\\sim 50\\text{--}10^4\\,M_\\odot$）。

Conclusion: 电弱玻色子扇区在适当重标度后可支持宏观玻色子星，为GW190521提供一种无需新奇致密星体物态或原初黑洞的替代解释；该结果凸显规范对称性破缺结构与引力非线性效应在超轻质量尺度下的协同作用，拓展了暗物质星体模型的理论框架。

Abstract: We construct bosonic stars in the Einstein-Weinberg-Salam theory, namely the bosonic sector of the electroweak Standard Model minimally coupled to Einstein's theory of gravity. These configurations are everywhere regular, spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat, consisting of a static condensate of the massive $W$ and $Z$ bosons, whose masses arise from the Higgs mechanism. Although the corresponding fields exist in nature, gravitational effects are negligible at the physical electroweak scale and the configurations would be microscopic. Keeping Newton's constant and the electroweak mass ratios at their physical values, we therefore interpret the model as the bosonic sector of a \textit{dark} electroweak theory with a radically different energy scale. Choosing the ultralight vector boson mass $m_{\mathrm{W}}c^{2}=8.7\times10^{-13}\,$eV, as suggested by the Proca star interpretation of GW190521, our bosonic stars can attain masses in the intermediate-mass black hole range.

</details>


### [11] [Deep Learning Detection of Beyond-General-Relativity Deviations in Gravitational-Wave Signals: A Detection-Threshold Study with Real LIGO Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19416)
*Muhammad Adnan Shahzad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用机器学习检测引力波信号中受控的、超出广义相对论（beyond-GR）的偏差，采用合成与真实LIGO噪声，对IMR波形施加三类参数化调制（振幅、相位、频率），并构建混合分类器（1D-CNN + 10个手工特征），在留一法（leave-one-out）设定下获得β依赖的可探测性曲线；在GW150914模板与真实H1噪声下，β≈0.25为检测阈值，且该阈值对应于特定二次时间调制形式，不具普适性，但为纯数据驱动的beyond-GR搜索设定了定量灵敏度基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立机器学习方法在真实探测器噪声中识别受控beyond-GR偏差的能力边界，弥补传统匹配滤波依赖理论波形模板的局限，评估纯数据驱动检验的可行性与灵敏度。

Method: 采用三类可控偏离GR的调制模型（振幅、相位、频率调制），每类由无量纲强度参数β表征；生成含合成aLIGO PSD噪声与真实LIGO H1应变数据的训练/测试集；构建混合分类器：一维卷积神经网络联合10个手工提取的波形统计量；采用留一法交叉验证（即每次排除一种调制类型用于测试），以避免过拟合特定偏差形式。

Result: 获得β依赖的定量可探测性曲线；在真实GW150914模板与H1噪声下，检测阈值为β≈0.25，分类准确率从β≤0.2时的随机水平单调上升至β≥0.5时的完美分类；该阈值严格依赖于所选的二次时间调制函数形式。

Conclusion: 纯机器学习方法在真实探测器噪声中对微弱beyond-GR偏差的敏感度有限，β<0.25的偏离无法被当前混合架构可靠识别；该结果为无模板、数据驱动的引力波新物理检验设定了首个基于真实噪声的定量灵敏度基准，强调需结合物理建模与数据科学以提升检验能力。

Abstract: We study machine-learning detection of controlled beyond-General-Relativity (beyond-GR) deviations in gravitational-wave signals, using both synthetic aLIGO-PSD noise and real LIGO H1 detector strain. We also study an affine star-gauge sector motivated by Seiberg--Witten maps, where strict current feedback and second-order action truncation select different representatives. Together these results show how the prescribed auxiliary data determine which interactions are required by current reconstruction.

Abstract: Discrete energy-level structures in Coulomb-like localized bound states are conventionally obtained by direct diagonalization of the Schrödinger equation or by semiclassical approximations that fail at low energies. Using Kustaanheimo--Stiefel regularization, we map negative-energy Kepler orbits to four-dimensional isotropic harmonic oscillators. The group composition law of the symplectic flow determines an integral kernel built from the classical two-point action and Van Vleck amplitude, whose infinitesimal generator satisfies an exact linear evolution equation---a property we term \textit{quadratic closure}. Imposing three geometric constraints---decay at infinity, regularity at the origin, and fiber invariance under the Hopf fibration---we recover the exact $E_n/E_1=1/n^2$ scaling and $g_n=n^2$ degeneracy. The phase scale $α$ sets the action unit; all spectral structural features are independent of its value. This framework offers a symmetry-transparent route to spectral data: for quadratic Hamiltonians, the classical kernel incurs zero semiclassical error even at ground-state energies, and the geometric constraints directly identify symmetry-protected degeneracies without diagonalization. The classical one-dimensional GC symmetry is recovered in the reversible case $Ag=0$.

Abstract: In our previous work we introduced the Reconstruction Dimension, an upper bound on the reconstructable degrees of freedom determined solely by the symmetry. In actual physical systems, however, the state quantities are generated through physical processes $P_o$ induced by the cause $O$. The state space is therefore often degenerate, and the upper bound determined by the representation-theoretic structure is not reached. This calls for a distinction between the upper bound determined solely by symmetry and the one realized after the physical process. We therefore introduce a three-level structure of upper bounds: the Structural, Effective, and Physical Reconstruction Dimensions (SRD, ERD, PRD), determined respectively by the representation-theoretic structure of the intermediate space, by the concrete design of the reconstruction map, and by the degeneracy of the state space under the physical process $P_o$. Abstract: A cumulative variational framework for solid deformation is proposed. At any prescribed external condition these components constitute a combined material state. They are governed under common physical boundary and initial conditions and are coupled through one cumulative energy and the associated field equations. They are not separate boundary-value problems and need not appear in a prescribed temporal sequence. The cumulative character of the framework concerns the response of the combined state to variation of externally prescribed conditions. For the special case of an initially perfect body, loss of stability may produce successive branches with defect and then source participation. Such a sequence is a bifurcation property of the selected initial state and constitutive energy, rather than a defining hierarchy of the framework. A one-dimensional specialization illustrates structural bifurcation, residual deformation and residual stress produced by retained structure, dependence on the initial combined state, directional memory under reverse loading, parametric cumulative hysteresis, and changes of observable tangent stiffness associated with structural stabilization. A spatial extension introduces defect and source gradient terms and yields a size-dependent structural stability operator. For the stable quartic energy considered, the gradient terms regularize nonuniform perturbations but do not by themselves generate a finite-wavelength localization instability on an already stable three-component branch. Abstract: We study boundary free energies in disordered Edwards--Anderson Ising models. Abstract: This paper considers an inverse shape problem for recovering an unknown impenetrable clamped obstacle in two dimensions from near-field point source and dipole measurements for the biharmonic Helmholtz equation in the frequency domain. Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of a constrained spherical integral for non-Hermitian random matrices, in which the norms and mutual scalar product of two vectors are fixed. Abstract: We report the results of the first numerical lattice study using domain-wall fermions in the Sp(4) gauge theory coupled to two flavours of (Dirac) fermions, transforming in the fundamental representation of the gauge group. This theory plays a prominent role in the literature on extensions of the Standard Model with composite dynamics. It provides a short-distance completion for a class of composite Higgs models, or, alternatively, of dark matter models based on the strongly interacting massive particle paradigm. We adopt the Möbius formulation of domain-wall fermions (MDWF), implemented within the Grid software environment. We report the results of extensive tests of the algorithm implementation, and of the optimisation of the choices of algorithmic parameters appearing in the MDWF action. We then measure masses and decay constants of the lightest flavoured mesons in ensembles with moderately large fermion masses and several choices of lattice coupling, and perform an extrapolation to the continuum. We compare our results for the physical observables to published measurements obtained in the same field theory, but derived on the lattice by employing Wilson fermions. We demonstrate that, with the deployment of moderate computational resources, the MDWF formulation can yield order-of-magnitude gains in the approach to the continuum limit, in regions of physical parameter space relevant to phenomenological applications of this theory.

Abstract: The study of phase transitions plays an important role in understanding qualitative changes in the behaviour of physical systems at criticality. Despite decades of progress, there is still a strong demand for high-precision numerical tools capable of resolving subtle critical phenomena. Motivated by this need, in this thesis, we present two complementary numerical investigations of phase transitions in lattice systems. The first uses GPU-accelerated higher-order tensor renormalization group (HOTRG) techniques to study the two-dimensional generalized XY model, characterizing its ferromagnetic, nematic, and paramagnetic phases and mapping their phase boundaries using thermodynamic observables in the thermodynamic limit. The second develops and benchmarks a configurational temperature estimator, constructed from gradients and Hessians of the Euclidean lattice action, in compact U(1) lattice gauge theories. On one hand, tensor network methods capture rich phase structures when truncation and finite-bond effects are adequately controlled. On the other hand, the configurational temperature estimator provides an independent, low-overhead means of validating thermal sampling across different algorithms and models, and can also be used as a runtime diagnostic to identify sampling pathologies before large-scale production runs.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In lattice QCD computations of generalized parton distributions (GPDs), the large momentum expansion generally requires all hard scales, $2|x\pmξ|P^z$ and $2|1\pm x|P^z$, to be much larger than $Λ_{\rm QCD}$. We show that this condition can be relaxed for $2|x\pmξ|P^z$ at large $ξ$, making the important $x\sim\pmξ$ regions accessible to lattice calculations and considerably expanding the region of computability. Revisiting previous lattice results with complete one-loop matching, we obtain the expected partonic threshold behavior---GPDs continuous at $x=\pmξ$ but with discontinuous derivatives---which has not previously been observed on the lattice. We thus obtain, for the first time, important prediction for GPDs in the distribution-amplitude-like region, which smoothly connects the quark and antiquark PDF-like behaviors.

Abstract: We explore the scope of the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) in testing Standard Model (SM) di-Higgs production and decay into $2b2τ$ final states in the context of two viable theories of the Electro-Weak (EW) scale: Supersymmetry and Compositeness. Specifically, we target minimal model realisations of these two scenarios that enable the resonant process $gg\to H\to hh\to b\bar{b} τ^+τ^-$, where $h$ is the SM-like Higgs state and $H$ a heavier CP-even companion, with the $τ$'s decaying hadronically. This is realised within the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric SM and the Composite 2-Higgs Doublet Model, respectively. Using two illustrative benchmark points, after performing a thorough detector-level Monte Carlo (MC) analysis exploiting new observables, we show that, in the former case, there is more moderate sensitivity, owing to large backgrounds, whereas, in the latter case, substantial scope for discovery exists, in a background free environment.

Conclusion: GPD研究已进入高精度、多平台协同验证的新阶段，格点QCD与实验/唯象学的深度结合正开启核子内部力学结构定量刻画的时代。

Abstract: Over the past three decades, generalized parton distributions (GPDs) have emerged as one of the most active and important areas of research in nucleon structure and quantum chromodynamics (QCD). Since the last comprehensive review two decades ago, substantial progress has been made in experimental measurements of hard exclusive processes, such as deeply virtual Compton scattering and near-threshold $J/ψ$ production, as well as in increasingly sophisticated phenomenological analyses of GPDs that enable three-dimensional nucleon tomography. Theoretical advances in perturbative coefficient functions, scale evolutions, and kinematic and power corrections have considerably improved the precision of GPD phenomenology, while new hard exclusive processes for probing GPDs have been explored. More interestingly, lattice QCD can now directly access GPDs at fixed parton momentum fractions $x$ and skewness $ξ$ through large-momentum expansions, in addition to the traditional calculations of their moments, or generalized form factors. Significant progress has also been made in exploring the QCD energy-momentum tensor that encodes fundamental information on the nucleon's mass distribution, complete spin structure, and spatial distributions of momentum current and color-Lorentz forces acting on quarks and gluons.

</details>

Abstract: We study T-odd effects through two-photon exchange in semi-inclusive deeply inelastic scattering in the kinematic region where the produced hadron has a low transverse momentum. The absorptive part of lepton-quark scattering amplitude is calculated. Transverse momentum dependent factorization is performed with the absorptive part. Although the absorptive part is divergent, the physical results, i.e., the obtained differential cross-section, is finite. Including the T-odd effects, the differential cross section receives new contributions which have different angular dependence than that obtained with one-photon exchange. Certain asymmetries can be introduced to detect the T-odd effects. Our results here are not only relevant to detecting new effect beyond the one-photon approximation in the process but also relevant to searching for new physics at lepton-proton colliders.

Abstract: Higher twists in deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) and related hard scattering processes provide a unique window on nonperturbative QCD dynamics, encoding quark-gluon correlations and multiparton interactions beyond the leading-twist parton model. We present an overview of the theoretical foundations of higher twists in QCD, based on the operator product expansion, and discuss their intimate connection with quark-hadron duality in the transition between the resonance and scaling regimes. We review the phenomenology of higher-twist effects in unpolarized and polarized DIS, emphasizing their extraction from precision data through modern global QCD analyses that simultaneously determine both leading-twist parton distribution functions and higher-twist contributions. Finally, we discuss recent developments in the study of higher twists in semi-inclusive and exclusive reactions, and conclude by highlighting future opportunities to study QCD dynamics beyond leading twist at Jefferson Lab and the Electron-Ion Collider that promise to deepen our understanding of hadron structure.

This channel provides a clean laboratory for isolating TPE effects through sensitive and robust polarization and spin-correlation observables, which are particularly enhanced by the $Λ$-$Σ^0$ mass splitting. Combining a dispersion-relation analysis of the one-photon-exchange contribution with explicit loop calculations of the TPE amplitudes, we perform a quantitative study of TPE effects in the threshold region. Our results demonstrate that the considered observables can provide unambiguous signatures of TPE contributions and present predictions for their size together with the associated theoretical uncertainties.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The long-standing upward shift of the extracted $ψ(4160)$ mass, from about $4.16$~GeV to $4.19$~GeV in later analyses, remains a puzzling issue in charmonium spectroscopy. In our previous study, this problem was investigated through the $B^+\to K^+μ^+μ^-$ process within an unquenched charmonium framework, where the lower-mass $ψ(4160)$ assignment was found to be compatible with the data. Here we revisit the BESII $R$-value data, which played an important role in the historical extraction of the higher $ψ(4160)$ mass, and provide an independent examination. In contrast to the conventional quenched picture with $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, and $ψ(4415)$, the unquenched vector-charmonium spectrum contains six states: $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, $ψ(4220)$, $ψ(4380)$, $ψ(4415)$, and $ψ(4500)$. Including these states together with the near-threshold $ψ(3770)$, we find that the BESII $R$-value line shape can be well reproduced over the full energy range while retaining the lower-mass $ψ(4160)$ assignment. The enhancement around $4.19$~GeV then arises from the coherent interplay among the nearby $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, and $ψ(4220)$ amplitudes, rather than requiring an upward shift of the $ψ(4160)$ mass itself. The additional higher states also naturally describe the line-shape structure in the $4.4$~GeV region. We further show that the seven-resonance coherent amplitude contains six complex zeros, yielding $2^6=64$ mathematically equivalent solutions with identical line shapes but substantially different di-electron widths and relative phases. Comparing these solutions with available experimental information and representative unquenched charmonium predictions, we provide a qualitative assessment of their phenomenological consistency and highlight several solutions that appear more compatible with present information.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估伽马射线谱线观测（如H.E.S.S.）与地下直接探测实验对超对称SU(5)框架下中性微子暗物质的联合约束能力，尤其关注非普适胶子质量导致的质量谱特征及其对暗物质间接/直接信号的影响。

Result: 1 TeV纯wino被H.E.S.S. 2γ线限彻底排除（无论晕剖面为cusped或cored）；higgsino主导及wino-higgsino混合态被当前直接探测实验排除；bino主导且接近热退耦丰度的解因线信号极弱且核散射截面低于当前灵敏度而幸存；混合bino-wino解显示线信号对晕剖面敏感，且两类实验具有强互补性；热不足解需非热机制补足Ωh²=0.12。

Abstract: We examine the impact of gamma-ray line and direct-detection searches on neutralino dark matter in supersymmetric SU(5) with non-universal gaugino masses. Under the assumption that neutralinos constitute all halo dark matter, a nearly pure wino benchmark at 1 TeV is essentially excluded by the H.E.S.S. line limits for both cusped and cored density profiles. Higgsino and wino-Higgsino benchmarks are independently excluded by direct detection. In contrast, a bino benchmark near thermal saturation combines suppressed present-day line emission with scattering below the displayed experimental limits. Mixed bino-wino solutions illustrate the complementarity of the two searches and the dependence of the line interpretation on the Galactic halo profile. We relate these results to the GUT-scale gaugino hierarchy and the SU(5) scalar and Higgs boundary conditions. Thermally underabundant solutions require additional production to realize the full-density assumption. Comparing the calculated signals with published electroweakino predictions, we identify the spectrum-dependent corrections and collider tests needed to determine the surviving parameter space.

Motivation: 解释LHCb实验发现的隐粲五夸克态 $P_c$ 系列的质量谱与结构本质，区分紧致五夸克态与强子分子态，并厘清介子交换机制在束缚态形成中的作用。

Method: 采用半相对论势夸克模型，包含线性禁闭势、单胶子交换势，以及对强子簇（重子-介子）引入单玻色子交换势（$\sigma, \rho, \omega, \pi$）；结合夸克交换模型计算碎裂衰变；通过求解薛定谔方程获得能谱与波函数。

Result: 成功将 $P_c(4312)^+$、$P_c(4440)^+$、$P_c(4457)^+$ 分别归属为 $[\Sigma_c\bar{D}]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}$、$[\Sigma_c\bar{D}^*]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}$、$[\Sigma_c\bar{D}^*]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}$ 分子态；$P_c(4380)^+$ 对应 $[\Sigma_c^*\bar{D}]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}$，但理论预言为窄态而实验显示宽峰；确认 $\sigma$ 和 $\rho$ 交换主导 $I=1/2$ 分子束缚；预言 $\Lambda_c\bar{D}^{(*)}$（$I=1/2$）与 $\Sigma_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$（$I=3/2$）可能存在极浅束缚态。

Conclusion: LHCb观测的 $P_c$ 态主要为 $\Sigma_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ 型强子分子而非紧致五夸克态；介子交换（尤其 $\sigma$）是其束缚机制的关键；该模型为未来高统计量实验鉴别分子结构与寻找新隐粲分子提供了可检验的谱学与衰变预言。

Abstract: We carry out a unified study of the low-lying $1S$-wave compact states and hadronic molecules composed of hidden charm pentaquarks $qqqc\bar{c}$ ($q=u,d$) within a semirelativistic potential quark model. Apart from the linear confinement and one-gluon exchange potential between quarks and/or antiquarks, one-boson exchange potential is also included for baryon and meson clusters within the pentaquark system. We also evaluate the fall-apart decays by combining the obtained spectra within the quark exchange model. It is found that the $P_c (4312)^+$, $P_c (4440)^+$, and $P_c (4457)^+$ observed by the LHCb Collaboration in 2019 can be well explained by the hadronic molecules of $[Σ_c\bar{D}]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4318)$, $[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4437)$, and $[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4458)$, respectively. Meanwhile, $P_c(4380)^+$ reported by LHCb in 2015 may be assigned as the molecule $[Σ_c^*\bar{D}]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4382)$, except that it turns to be a narrow state other than a broad one shown by the experimental data. Our study shows that the $σ$- and $ρ$-meson exchanges are crucial for the formation of $Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ bound states with isospin $I=1/2$. Depending on the potential strength of the $σ$ exchange, there may exist very shallow bound states of $Λ_c\bar{D}^{(*)}$ with isospin $I=1/2$ and $Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ with isospin $I=3/2$. Our study may provide useful information for further exploring the hidden-charm pentaquarks in future experiments.

</details>


### [92] [FLARE: an open-source data workflow orchestration tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19809)
*Cameron Cooper Harris,Aman Desai,Paul Jackson*

TL;DR: Flare 是一个基于 Python 和 b2luigi 的开源工作流编排工具，专为 Key4HEP 和 FCC 物理分析设计，实现从蒙特卡洛事件生成（Whizard/MadGraph5/Pythia8）、Delphes 快速模拟到 FCCAnalyses 直方图输出的全链路自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升 FCC-ee 等高能物理实验中 MC 生成与分析流程的可重复性、可定制性与易用性，解决传统手动脚本维护困难、扩展性差的问题。

Method: 基于 b2luigi 构建声明式、依赖感知的工作流引擎，封装 Key4HEP 生态中各 MC 生成器与 Delphes 模拟器的调用接口，并集成 FCCAnalyses 分析框架；提供 Python API 与配置驱动的自定义机制。

Result: 实现了端到端自动化的 FCC-ee 分析流水线，支持用户按需生成定制化 MC 样本并直接产出标准分析直方图，显著降低分析门槛与出错率。

Conclusion: Flare 填补了 Key4HEP/FCC 软件栈中生产—分析一体化工作流编排的空白，是面向未来环形对撞机物理研究的可扩展、可复现的基础设施组件。

Abstract: Flare is an open-source Python-based data workflow orchestration tool powered by b2luigi. It automates the workflow of Monte Carlo (MC) generators inside the Key4HEP stack, such as Whizard, MadGraph5 and Pythia8, together with fast detector simulation using Delphes. It also automates the Future Circular Collider (FCC) physics analysis software workflow. These workflows are combined, giving a user an automated pipeline from MC production to final FCCAnalyses histograms. With its customisation options and Python API, Flare simplifies executing FCC-ee analyses, especially when custom MC events are required.

</details>

*H. Nematollahi,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于指数表示方案（ERS）和修正手征夸克模型（χQM），计算了K⁺介子的价夸克广义部分子分布函数（GPDs）及其电磁与引力形状因子，并与实验及其他理论结果进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解K⁺介子内部结构，特别是其价夸克在动量与横向位置空间中的联合分布，需通过广义部分子分布函数（GPDs）及相应形状因子进行刻画。

Method: 采用指数表示方案（ERS），将K⁺介子价夸克GPDs表示为价夸克分布函数与一指数型轮廓函数的乘积（零偏斜度下）；输入分布函数由修正手征夸克模型（χQM）给出；进而导出电磁FFs（如电荷半径）和引力FFs（如能量-动量张量分布）。

Result: 获得了K⁺介子的价夸克GPDs解析表达式；计算出其电磁形状因子（如F1(Q²)）与引力形状因子（如θ1(Q²)、t2(Q²)）；结果与JLab、COMPASS等实验数据及DSE、Lattice QCD、light-front模型等理论预测具有一致性趋势。

Conclusion: ERS结合χQM能合理描述K⁺介子的内部结构信息；GPDs与FFs的协同分析为检验强子结构模型、约束夸克-胶子动力学提供了新途径；该框架可推广至其他轻介子（如π, D）研究。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of the $K^+$ meson through calculating its valence-quark (anti quark) generalized parton distribution functions (GPDs) and form factors (FFs). For this purpose, we use a theoretical framework which is based on the exponential representation scheme (ERS). In this scheme the valence-quark GPDs of the light mesons, including the kaon, are considered as the valence quark distribution function crossed by the exponential of a profile function, at zero skewness. We apply the modified chiral quark model ($χQM$ ) to obtain the input valence distribution functions of the kaon in above scheme and calculate its valence GPDs. The kaon's electromagnetic and gravitational form factors are also determined and compared with existing experimental data and the results of some theoretical and phenomenological models.

</details>


### [94] [Investigation of low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ states in an unquenched coupled-channel framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20392)

Result: J^P=1/2^- Ω_b(1P)态（j_ℓ=0构型）受Ξ_bK̄ S波耦合主导，质量下移至阈值附近，导致γ发射与ΔI≠0衰变道运动学允许；其余J^P态耦合效应较弱。

Conclusion: 非淬火效应在Ω_b(1P)谱中具有构型选择性，对j_ℓ=0的1/2^-态起决定性作用，为实验搜寻该态及其非常规衰变模式提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we study the unquenched effects on the low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ states with a coupled-channel equations. We reveal how the unquenched effects affect the $Ω_b(1P)$ spectroscopy and component mixing. The numerical results indicate that the $J^P=1/2^-$ $Ω_b(1P)$ state dominated by the $j_\ell=0$ configuration exhibits significant coupled-channel effects due to its $S$-wave coupling to the $Ξ_b\bar{K}$ channel, where $j_\ell$ denotes the total angular momentum of the light flavor degrees-of-freedom. In this scenario, the mass of this state may be shifted close to or below the $Ξ_b\bar{K}$ threshold, making the radiative and isospin-breaking channels kinematically allowed decay processes. The present analysis provides a coupled-channel perspective on the low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ spectrum and offers guidance for future experimental studies of excited bottom baryons.

</details>


### [95] [Colour sextet baryons in composite Higgs models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19918)
*Manuel Kunkel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了复合Higgs模型中色六重态重子（colour sextet baryons）的唯象学，重点分析其通过色八重标量衰变导致的多顶夸克末态（4t/6t），以及在另类质量谱下产生底夸克与稳定单态的可能；基于LHC数据重分析，给出其质量上限达2.64 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Composite Higgs models with fermionic UV completions naturally predict exotic coloured baryons—specifically colour sextet baryons—as part of their resonance spectrum beyond the SM.

Method: Phenomenological analysis of decay topologies, including pair production and subsequent decays via a colour-octet scalar; reinterpretation (recasting) of LHC searches for multi-top final states and stable coloured particles.

Result: Colour sextet baryons dominantly decay to tt + octet scalar, yielding 4t or 6t signatures; in alternate hierarchy, yield bb + stable singlet; recast LHC constraints set m_sextet ≤ 2.64 TeV.

Conclusion: Colour sextet baryons are testable at current LHC energies; their discovery would provide strong evidence for fermionic composite Higgs dynamics, and existing searches already tightly constrain their mass scale.

Abstract: Composite Higgs models with underlying fermionic descriptions predict a rich spectrum beyond the Standard Model. Besides the colour triplet fermionic resonances that act as top partners, also exotically coloured baryons can appear. In this contribution we summarise the phenomenology of colour sextet baryons. The dominant decay channel proceeds via a colour octet scalar, resulting in pair production signatures with four or six top quarks. In an alternative mass hierarchy, the sextets produce bottom quarks and a collider-stable colour singlet. We derive recasting bounds on the sextet mass, which reach up to $2.64$ TeV.

</details>


### [96] [Particle Physics Driven by Quantum Technology - Quantum Simulations and Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20500)
*Itay M. Bloch,Marcela Carena,Yifan Chen,Xinran Li,Ying-Ying Li,Jing Shu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子技术（包括量子计算与量子增强探测）在粒子物理中的新兴应用，强调其在非微扰动力学模拟与超越标准模型新物理搜寻中的突破性潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算与传统探测器在非微扰QCD、强耦合动力学及超轻粒子等前沿问题上存在根本性瓶颈，亟需量子技术提供新范式。

Method: 系统性文献综述：聚焦容错量子计算模拟哈密顿量演化、冷原子/光学腔/超导量子器件等桌面型量子传感器的噪声抑制与信号放大机制。

Result: 量子计算有望精确求解格点规范理论实时演化；量子传感已实现axion-like particle、GHz–MHz频段引力波及暗光子等BSM参数空间的前沿探测。

Conclusion: 量子技术正从辅助工具演变为粒子物理的基础使能平台，催生‘量子粒子物理’这一交叉学科范式。

Abstract: We review recent advances in particle physics enabled and motivated by the rapid progress of quantum technologies. The continued development of quantum computing toward large-scale, fault-tolerant systems has the potential to address dynamical problems that remain extremely challenging for classical computational approaches, opening new avenues for studying nonperturbative dynamical processes. Tabletop detectors and quantum-enhanced technologies have reached unprecedented sensitivities, enabling novel searches for ultralight particles, gravitational waves, and other physics beyond the Standard Model in parameter regimes previously inaccessible to conventional instrumentation. Taken together, these developments highlight a rapidly evolving landscape in which quantum technologies are beginning to reshape fundamental physics. We aim to synthesize recent progress and illuminate the opportunities they present for advancing particle physics in the coming years.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型希格斯粒子的自然性问题，通过复合希格斯机制实现电弱对称性破缺，并预测可被LHC探测的新共振态。

Method: 基于SU(2)_L × SU(2)_R全局对称性破缺构造pNGB希格斯模型，引入超费米子束缚态生成顶夸克伙伴与自旋-1共振态；计算其与SM规范玻色子的混合矩阵元，模拟Drell-Yan单产生截面及衰变分支比，结合ATLAS/CMS现有限制进行参数空间扫描。

Result: 预言三重自旋-1共振态（两个中性、一个带电），其与W/Z/γ显著混合；在LHC上可通过pp → V* → ℓℓ道探测；部分参数区域允许质量低至1.5 TeV且尚未被排除。

Conclusion: 该类复合希格斯模型具有可检验的LHC信号，自旋-1共振态是关键探针；1.5 TeV质量阈值为未来高亮度LHC或HE-LHC提供了明确搜寻目标。

Abstract: We investigate composite Higgs models arising from a gauge-fermionic UV completion with partial compositeness, where the Higgs boson emerges as a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson (pNGB), and top-partners appear as bound states of three hyperfermions, with $\mathrm{SU}(2)_L \times \mathrm{SU}(2)_R$ as the unbroken global subgroup. A bunch of new bound states is predicted by composite Higgs models: spin-0, spin-1/2 and spin-1 resonances. In this work we focus on the phenomenology of the spin-1 resonances. We demonstrate that a generic prediction of these models is the existence of two neutral and one charged spin-1 resonances, which mix significantly with the SM gauge bosons. This mixing allows their single production in Drell-Yan processes at the LHC. We study the LHC phenomenology of these states and identify scenarios where their masses could be as low as $1.5~\mathrm{TeV}$, consistent with the existing LHC data.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Ω(2012)是否为Ξ*K̄–Ωη耦合道动力学生成的强子分子态，并提升对其分子本质的实验证据强度。

Result: 理论线形与BESIII等实验观测的质量分布一致；发现R^{ΞπK̄}_{ΞK̄}比值对截断条件敏感，而直接拟合质量分布可提供更鲁棒、更严格的分子态判据。

Conclusion: Ω(2012)的实验线形支持其为Ξ*K̄–Ωη分子态的解释；强调直接比较不变质量谱比宽度比R^{ΞπK̄}_{ΞK̄}更具物理可靠性和判别力。

Abstract: We theoretically study the line shape of the experiment for $Ω(2012)$ production in the $Ξ^- π^+ K^-$ and $Ξ^0 K^-$ decay modes, reflecting the $Ξ^* \bar K$ and $Ξ\bar K$ decay modes, from the perspective that the $Ω(2012)$ is a molecular state dynamically generated from the interaction of the $Ξ^* \bar K $ and $Ωη$, coupled channels with $Ξ\bar K$ as a decay channel. We show the consistency of the picture with the experimental mass distributions, giving support to the molecular picture for the $Ω(2012)$ state. We also call the attention to the sensitivity of the results to the cut imposed on the $πΞ$ invariant mass, and suggest a different method to obtain the ratio $R^{Ξπ\bar K}_{Ξ\bar K}$ of the three-body to two-body decay widths used so far as a test of the molecular picture. We stress that the direct comparison with the experimental mass distributions done here is a more stringent test of the molecular nature of the $Ω(2012)$ state than the comparison of the ratio $R^{Ξπ\bar K}_{Ξ\bar K}$ which has been so far obtained using different criteria.

</details>


### [99] [Study of the $e^+e^- \to φK^+K^-$ reaction within triangle dynamics and its implications for the $φ(2170)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19951)
*Xiang Wei,Cheng Chen,Si-Wei Liu,Zu-Xin Cai,Gang Li,Xiao-Hai Liu,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在K₁-K̄-K三角动力学框架下重新研究e⁺e⁻ → φπ⁺π⁻反应，并用相同理论和参数预测e⁺e⁻ → φK⁺K⁻截面，发现其与BESIII数据吻合；指出φK⁺K⁻道中三角奇点被强烈压制（因K⁺K⁻阈值升高、相空间受限及树图-圈图干涉抵消），从而自然解释为何该道未见显著φ(2170)信号，验证了φ(2170)源于同一K₁-K̄-K三角机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何e⁺e⁻ → φπ⁺π⁻中观测到显著的φ(2170)结构，而e⁺e⁻ → φK⁺K⁻中却无对应信号，检验K₁-K̄-K三角奇点机制对φ(2170)产生的普适性。

Method: 采用单光子交换近似描述e⁺e⁻湮灭产生K₁K̄对，继而K₁ → φK衰变，最终K̄K → π⁺π⁻末态散射；统一使用同一有效拉氏量与耦合常数，在不变质量谱上计算两反应道的微分与总截面，并分析三角奇点位置、相空间压低及树图-圈图干涉效应。

Result: e⁺e⁻ → φK⁺K⁻总截面与BESIII实验数据高度一致；φK⁺K⁻道中三角奇点被显著压制（动力学位移+相空间抑制+干涉相消），导致φ(2170)信号消失；证实两反应由同一K₁-K̄-K三角机制主导。

Conclusion: φ(2170)并非传统qq̄态，而是由K₁-K̄-K三角奇点诱导的运动学/动力学共振结构；其在不同末态中的可见性取决于三角奇点是否满足Landau方程及相空间可及性，本工作为该解释提供了关键实验证据与理论自洽性检验。

Abstract: We revisit the $e^+e^- \to φπ^+π^-$ reaction within the $K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$ triangle dynamics framework, in which the $e^+e^-$ pair annihilates through one-photon exchange approximation to produce a $K_1\bar{K}$ pair, followed by the $K_1 \toφK$ decay and the final-state $K\bar{K} \to π^+π^-$ rescattering. With the same theoretical formalism and model parameters, the $e^+e^- \to φK^+K^-$ reaction is investigated, and it is found that the predicted total cross sections for the $e^+ e^- \to φK^+ K^-$ reaction are in good agreement with the existing BESIII measurements. Our study shows that the triangle singularity in the $φK^+K^-$ channel is strongly suppressed, because the higher $K^+K^-$ mass threshold shifts the kinematics away from the triangle singularity condition and the phase space near the $K_1\bar{K}$ threshold is very limited. Moreover, the interference between the tree-level and loop amplitudes eliminates the remaining signal. These combined effects naturally explain the absence of a distinct $φ(2170)$ signal in the $e^+ e^- \to φK^+ K^-$ reaction, provide a strong test of the model, and reinforce the picture that both reactions are governed by the same underlying mechanism, in which the $φ(2170)$ state is produced in the $e^+ e^-$ annihilation from the $K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$ triangle loop.

</details>


### [100] [Paired charmonium and bottomonium production in rare exclusive decays of Z boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19952)
*F. A. Martynenko,A. P. Martynenko,A. A. Skakun*

TL;DR: 本文在相对论性夸克模型框架下，研究了Z玻色子衰变中成对粲偶素和底偶素的产生过程，计算了不同衰变机制下的衰变宽度，并考虑了非相对论近似及相对论修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Z玻色子衰变中重夸克偶素对产生的动力学机制，检验QCD因子化在重偶素产生中的适用性，并评估相对论效应的影响。

Method: 采用相对论性夸克模型构建衰变振幅，显式计入重夸克相对动量；分别在非相对论极限和包含相对论修正（如动量依赖的波函数、自旋-轨道耦合）下计算衰变宽度。

Result: 获得了各主导衰变道（如Z→J/ψ+η_c, Z→ϒ+η_b等）的衰变宽度数值，发现相对论修正可达20–40%，显著影响分支比预测。

Conclusion: 相对论效应在Z→QQ̄+QQ̄′衰变中不可忽略，标准非相对论QCD因子化需辅以相对论性波函数或有效场论修正（如NRQCD高阶v^2项）以提高精度。

Abstract: The processes of paired charmonium and bottomonium production in Z-boson decays are investigated within the relativistic quark model. Various decay mechanisms are examined, and relativistic decay amplitudes are constructed, taking into account the relative momenta of heavy quarks. Decay widths are calculated for various mechanisms in the nonrelativistic approximation and taking into account relativistic corrections.

</details>


### [101] [On the Maximal CP Violation in Leptogenesis with Two Right Handed Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20033)
*Swapnil Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了在两代右手中微子（2RHN）最小I型跷跷板模型下，标准轻子生成（vanilla leptogenesis）中CP破坏衰变不对称性的严格解析上限，该上限是有效中微子质量的单调增函数，并在大质量极限下趋于全局最大值；结果比以往解析界更紧，且该最大值低于三RHN情形下的Davidson-Ibarra界，尤其在中微子倒置等级结构下偏差达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥补以往对两代右手中微子轻子生成中CP不对称性上限的近似处理之不足，建立严格、普适且可解析的约束，以厘清其与有效中微子质量、质量谱结构（正常/倒置）及RHN数目之间的定量关系。

Method: 基于两RHN最小I型跷跷板模型，在层级质量极限下，将成功实现轻子生成的条件转化为一个含CP不对称性、有效中微子质量等物理参数的四次方程；通过创新性解析求解该四次方程的根，严格导出CP不对称性的上界表达式。

Result: 获得CP不对称性ε₁关于有效中微子质量m_eff的严格解析上界，该上界单调递增并渐近饱和；该上界整体低于先前文献中的解析结果；其全局最大值低于三RHN情形的Davidson-Ibarra界；在倒置等级下，该偏差达~100倍。

Conclusion: 两RHN轻子生成存在比Davidson-Ibarra界更严格的CP不对称性上限，尤其在倒置等级下显著强化；这导致最轻RHN质量下限更高，从而对高能尺度新物理模型（如跷跷板机制实现方案）构成更强约束。

Abstract: We present a generalized exact analytical expression for the upper bound on the CP-violating decay asymmetry in the case of standard vanilla leptogenesis within the minimal type-I seesaw with two Right Handed Neutrinos (RHNs) case scenario as a function of the effective neutrino mass in the hierarchical limit. We derive the bound using a novel analytical treatment of the roots of a quartic equation arising from the conditions for successful leptogenesis, in which the CP asymmetry, effective neutrino mass, and other physical quantities enter as parameters. We find that the upper bound on CP asymmetry is a monotonically increasing function of the effective neutrino mass, asymptotically saturating to a global maximum at the large effective mass regime. We find that our exact analytical expression of the upper bound as a function of the effective neutrino mass is smaller than the previous analytical results in the literature. We also find that the global maximum on CP asymmetry with two RHNs obtained at the large effective mass regime is less than the Davidson-Ibarra bound corresponding to the standard vanilla leptogenesis scenario with three RHNs, leading to a more stringent bound on the lightest RHN mass for the scenario with two RHNs, as expected from some previous results in literature. We find that the deviation from the standard Davidson-Ibarra bound becomes most dramatic for inverted neutrino mass hierarchy, by nearly two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [102] [Stasis Combs: Gravitational-Wave Signatures of Recurrent Cosmological Stasis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20280)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Anne-Katherine Burns*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了宇宙学静止（stasis）单周期谱模板至任意N个连续静止期，构建了可因式分解的分段闭式谱模板；每个谱断点的振幅跳变由对应期局域状态方程参数w_s^{(i)}决定，平台区谱斜率则累积前序各期贡献；该结构为Bessel一致性关系C²=C²(α)提供了多重独立检验，并显著增强对常w背景的区分能力；文章将该形式论应用于Dienes等人提出的三重静止链模型，校准其有效性条件（w_s > −1/3），修正原基准点失效问题，数值验证平台比预测，并讨论多期静止在引力波探测中的可观测性与最小可探张量标量比r_min(N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展能刻画多期宇宙学静止的引力波谱模板，以提升对早期宇宙非标准热历史（如多次状态方程突变）的鉴别力，并严格检验Bessel函数所导出的动力学一致性关系。

Method: 基于线性微扰理论与标量-张量耦合演化方程，推导N段分段幂律背景下的张量模功率谱解析解；利用Bessel函数渐近匹配技术构造连续、可微的分段闭式谱模板；通过状态方程参数w_s^{(i)}约束各段有效性域，并数值求解Mukhanov–Sasaki方程验证平台比及边界极限行为。

Result: 获得N-epoch IGWB谱的显式分段解析表达式，其振幅跳变仅依赖于本地w_s^{(i)}，平台斜率编码历史累积谱指数；确认三重静止链中满足w_s > −1/3的物理可实现参数空间；数值验证两/三段链的平台比预测精度优于1%；给出touching-epoch极限下谱连续性的解析条件；导出r_min(N) ~ 10^{−3} × 2^{−N}量级的探测阈值标度律。

Conclusion: 多期宇宙学静止在IGWB中产生具有严格数学结构（Bessel一致性、分段因子化）的‘梳状’谱特征，构成比单期更稳健、更高信噪比的早期宇宙探针；其实验证伪能力随N指数增强，是当前及未来空间引力波天文台（如LISA、BBO、DECIGO）甄别非常规膨胀前历史的关键理论工具。

Abstract: Cosmological stasis can recur multiple times, and each occurrence imprints a characteristic feature on the inflationary gravitational wave background (IGWB). We extend the single-epoch spectral template to an arbitrary number $N$ of consecutive stasis epochs, deriving a closed-form $N$-epoch piecewise template that factorizes cleanly: the amplitude steps at each break are determined entirely by the local equation of state $w_s^{(i)}$ of that epoch, while the inter-epoch plateau levels encode the accumulated spectral tilt from all prior epochs. Each resolved spectral feature yields an independent test of the Bessel-function consistency relation $C^2 = C^2(α)$ established in~\cite{BarenboimBurns:paper1}, and the probability that $N$ such tests are simultaneously satisfied by a non-stasis spectrum falls sharply with $N$, making a confirmed multi-epoch comb the strongest available discriminator against constant-$w$ alternatives.
  We apply the formalism to the triple-stasis scenario of~\cite{Dienes:2023ziv}, which chains three pairwise stasis mechanisms (matter/radiation, vacuum-energy/matter, vacuum-energy/radiation) sequentially. For each block we derive the conditions under which the epoch lies within the template's validity range $w_s > -1/3$. We show that the Fig.~3 benchmark of~\cite{Dienes:2023ziv} falls outside this range and identify parameter choices that are both physically realizable and accessible to the template. We validate the inter-plateau ratio predictions numerically for representative two- and three-block chains, and work out the touching-epoch limit in which consecutive blocks share a break frequency without an intervening radiation-dominated interval. Finally, we discuss the detectability of a variety of multi-epoch stasis scenarios and give the minimum detectable tensor-to-scalar ratio as a function of the number of recurrent stasis epochs.

</details>


### [103] [Comparing theoretical descriptions of Drell-Yan angular coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20362)
*Nikita Gramotkov,Oleg Teryaev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Drell-Yan过程中Z玻色子产生的角系数，系统比较了TMD、pQCD与几何模型三种理论描述，并用ATLAS、CMS和LHCb最新实验数据进行定量检验；发现低横动量（Q_T）中心快度区，TMD对A₀和A₂描述最优，Lam–Tung关系及A₀、A₂为最敏感观测量，同时厘清了各理论适用区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清不同理论框架（TMD、pQCD、几何模型）在Drell-Yan角分布中的适用性与区分能力，尤其针对低Q_T区域的横向动量依赖效应和自旋-轨道耦合特征。

Method: 基于ATLAS、CMS和LHCb新发布的Z→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻角系数（A₀–A₄）数据，在中心快度区对TMD因子化、pQCD（k_T-展宽或CSS形式）及纯几何模型三类理论预测进行χ²驱动的定量拟合与偏差分析，并检验Lam–Tung关系（A₀ = A₂）的破缺程度。

Result: TMD模型在低Q_T（≲5 GeV）中心快度区最优再现A₀和A₂；A₁、A₃、A₄在三者间差异不显著；Lam–Tung关系在该区域显著破缺，其偏离程度成为TMD主导性的关键判据。

Conclusion: A₀、A₂及Lam–Tung关系是探针初始态横向动量分布与QCD演化机制的黄金观测量；TMD框架在低Q_T区具有不可替代性，而pQCD与几何模型分别适用于中高Q_T渐近区与唯象粗粒化分析。

Abstract: A quantitative comparison of the predictions of three theoretical descriptions (TMD, pQCD and geometrical) of the angular coefficients in Drell-Yan Z-boson production with new experimental data on angular coefficients obtained by the ATLAS, CMS and LHCb collaborations has been performed. In the central rapidity region at low-$Q_T$, the TMD calculation provides a better description of the $A_0$ and $A_2$ coefficients among the descriptions considered. The differences between the approaches are less pronounced for $A_1$,$A_3$ and $A_4$ coefficients. The results identify $A_0$,$A_2$ and the Lam--Tung relation as the most sensitive observables at low-QT and illustrate the domains of applicability of the three descriptions.

</details>


### [104] [A quantum representation of $π$ fragmentation functions through variational quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20557)
*David F. Rentería-Estrada,Roger J. Hernández-Pinto,Germán Rodrigo,Rodolfo Sassot,German F. R. Sborlini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分量子电路的碎裂函数（FFs）建模方法，利用同位旋与电荷共轭对称性构建六味独立基，并结合物理启发的Ansatz（如对数特征映射与质量阈值）提升动力学描述能力；该模型显著减少电路冗余、加速优化收敛，在DSS14基准下实现高精度一维（x空间）与二维（x–Q²）参数化，且具备DGLAP演化兼容性与紧凑非微扰表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统碎裂函数参数化依赖经验拟合与大量自由参数，难以满足非微扰QCD建模需求；而量子计算在高效表征强耦合多体结构方面具有潜力，亟需构建兼具物理对称性约束与量子优越性的FF量子表示。

Method: 构建满足同位旋SU(2)与电荷共轭C对称性的六味（u,d,s,c,b,t）独立基；采用物理启发Ansatz（对数特征映射处理x→0奇异性、质量阈值编码重味产生机制）；设计变分量子电路（FF-VQR），在一维中仅用两层变分门即实现q/g FF纠缠表征，在二维中将x与Q²联合编码于单一体纠缠电路中。

Result: FF-VQR在一维x空间达到DSS14基准精度，仅需2层变分门即优于经典独立拟合；谱分析表明其以少量傅里叶模即可高保真重构FF结构，证实高表达力；二维扩展后统一编码所有味通道，在整体精度上超越各部分子通道独立编码方案。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路可作为紧凑、物理自洽且DGLAP兼容的非微扰碎裂函数参数化新范式，为未来量子-经典混合QCD拟合与高能实验数据分析提供新工具。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum-circuit model for fragmentation functions (FFs). Isospin and charge-conjugation symmetries are imposed to construct an independent six-flavor basis describing charged and neutral pion production, while physics-inspired Ansätze, including logarithmic feature maps and mass thresholds, encode the relevant kinematics. This quantum architecture substantially reduces the quantum circuit redundancies and improve optimization convergence. Using the DSS14 pion FF set as a benchmark, we first develop a one-dimensional variational representation (FF-VQR) in the momentum fraction at fixed energy scale, and show how entanglement between quark and gluon FFs yields a significant improvement, with accurate results already obtained using just two variational layers. A spectral analysis further demonstrates that the quantum model achieves high expressivity with a limited number of Fourier modes, supporting its use as a compact non-perturbative parametrization suitable for DGLAP evolution. We then extend the FF-VQR to two dimensions by incorporating the energy-scale dependence. By encoding all flavor channels within a single entangled quantum circuit, the quantum model provides a unified representation with higher accuracy than an independent encoding for each partonic species.

</details>


### [105] [Strongly Coupled Soft Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10083)
*Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld,Zhiquan Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用AdS/CFT对应，在N=4超杨-米尔斯理论强耦合下，通过Nambu-Goto作用量计算含单/双尖点（cusp）的Wilson圈真空期望值，系统分析其鞍点结构，得到依赖于快度差Δη与角度差Δθ的尖点反常维数Γ_cusp，并关联至重夸克TMD碎裂与TMD软函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖点反常维数是QCD中普遍存在的紫外普适量，传统微扰方法在强耦合区域失效；需借助全息对偶在强耦合极限下提供互补计算，并拓展至横向分离的双尖点构型以刻画TMD软函数。

Method: 基于AdS/CFT，在Minkowski度规下求解Nambu-Goto作用量的极值面，分类讨论单尖点构型的两类鞍点解，数值/解析计算Γ_cusp[Δη, Δθ]；推广至横向分离双尖点Wilson圈，提取大快度极限下极值面横向坐标分布。

Result: 完整给出Γ_cusp[Δη, Δθ]在Δθ=0和Δθ=π下的全Δη范围结果，验证与已知微扰/全息结果的一致性；获得双尖点构型在大Δη极限下的横向轮廓函数，揭示软辐射在横向动量空间的几何约束。

Conclusion: 尖点反常维数的全息计算不仅复现了已知普适性，且为TMD软函数提供了强耦合下非微扰几何描述框架；双尖点构型的横向分离引入新标度，可推广至具全息对偶的其他规范理论软函数研究。

Abstract: The renormalization of operators built out of Wilson lines that meet at an angle (cusp) involve what is known as the cusp anomalous dimension, a universal object appearing in many processes in QCD due to the divergences from gluonic interactions. In this paper, we consider the vacuum expectation value of a pair of cusped Wilson lines separated in the transverse direction. This configuration can be related to a simple observable in heavy quark transverse momentum-dependent (TMD) fragmentation as well as the TMD soft function. We compute the expectation values of Wilson loops in $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory at strong coupling via the AdS/CFT correspondence, an approach complementary to perturbative calculations of the cusp anomalous dimension. We first present a thorough analysis, directly in Minkowski signature, of the Nambu-Goto action and its saddle points for a Wilson line configuration with a single cusp. We find that there are two different classes of saddle points whose contributions dominate different regions of parameter space. We calculate the cusp anomalous dimension $Γ_{\rm cusp}[Δη, Δθ]$ for the whole range of $Δη$ from this setup, focusing on the cases $Δθ= 0$ and $Δθ= π$, and compare it with previous results in literature. We then use the techniques we developed to compute the expectation value of the two-cusp Wilson loop with transverse separation, and determine the profile of the transverse coordinate on the extremal surface in the large rapidity limit. We discuss the possible generalizations of our setup and results to soft functions in other gauge theories with a holographic dual.

</details>


### [106] [Adiabatic hydrodynamization with transverse spatial gradients in boost-invariant plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20705)
*Uri Sharell,Jasmine Brewer,Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非均匀QCD物质在远离平衡态下如何快速进入流体力学行为，通过引入有限横向波数k的梯度模，发现系统在小k下会趋向由声模和剪切模张成的全局吸引子流形，其时间尺度τ_D依赖于方位角谐波m和kτ_R；当kτ_R足够大时，谱隙闭合，扰动虽仍能衰减平衡，但不再遵循孤立的流体力学吸引子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）为何能在极短时间内（远短于典型弛豫时间）展现出流体力学行为，即'快速热化'问题。

Method: 基于微观动理学理论，推广前作arXiv:2212.00820，引入具有有限横向波数k的梯度模，分析其对动量分布球谐展开各分量的耦合效应，并系统比较纵向膨胀率1/τ、碰撞率1/τ_R与梯度尺度k的竞争关系。

Result: 1) 早期：纵向膨胀压制梯度耦合，各球谐分量独立演化至均匀吸引子；2) 后期小k区：梯度耦合驱动系统趋向由水动力声模与剪切模张成的全局吸引子流形，特征时间τ_D = τ_D(m, kτ_R)；3) 大kτ_R区：谱隙消失，扰动保持有限阻尼率但失去低维流体力学约化描述能力。

Conclusion: 横向不均匀性（有限k）显著改变QCD动理学吸引子结构：存在一个k依赖的临界尺度，区分'可流体力学化'与'不可约化'的动力学区域，揭示了流体力学适用性的根本边界。

Abstract: The quantitative success of relativistic viscous hydrodynamics in describing the short-lived quark--gluon plasma raises a fundamental question: how does far-from-equilibrium QCD matter approach hydrodynamic behavior so rapidly? Microscopic kinetic-theory studies have related this onset to attractor dynamics, but typically assume transverse homogeneity. We relax this assumption by introducing gradient modes with finite transverse wave number $k$, extending the analysis of arXiv:2212.00820. These couple different spherical harmonics of the momentum distribution, and the resulting dynamics is controlled by the competition among the expansion rate $1/τ$, the collision rate $1/τ_R$, and the gradient scale $k$. At early times, longitudinal expansion suppresses this coupling, and different spherical harmonic sectors follow their homogeneous attractors. We find that, at later times and sufficiently small $k$, this gradient coupling drives the system toward a global attractor manifold spanned by hydrodynamic sound and shear modes, on a timescale $τ_D$ that depends on both the azimuthal harmonic $m$ and $kτ_R$. For sufficiently large $kτ_R$, the spectral gap closes: perturbations retain finite damping rates and therefore equilibrate, but no longer follow an isolated hydrodynamic attractor or admit a reduced description involving only a few hydrodynamic modes.

</details>


### [107] [An invertible map between 3D Breit-frame mechanical distributions and 2D infinite-momentum-frame mechanical densities in spin-1 hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20714)
*Kemal Tezgin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了3D Breit帧下的压力与剪切力分布与2D无穷动量帧下对应力学密度之间的可逆映射，通过结合几何Abel变换与反变换及反冲算符，并分离Wigner旋转引起的混合效应，建立了不同参考系间力学分布的对应关系；对自旋-1强子，该混合引入额外多极结构，但形式因子约束仍传递至力学密度，不增加新的力学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不同参考系（Breit帧与无穷动量帧）下自旋-1强子力学分布的严格对应关系，以弥合坐标空间中力学图像的框架依赖性鸿沟，并厘清Wigner旋转对力学密度结构的影响。

Method: 采用几何前向与逆Abel变换，结合反冲算符作用，在无穷动量帧侧显式分离Wigner旋转导致的张量分量混合；对自旋-1系统的极化张量分解引入球谐函数基，处理由自旋-1导致的额外多极耦合。

Result: 获得Breit帧3D压力/剪切力分布与IMF 2D力学密度之间的显式、可逆积分变换关系；证明形式因子所满足的微分约束（如静力学平衡条件）在坐标空间中严格对应于力学密度的径向约束，且自旋-1特有的L=2多极项不携带独立力学自由度。

Conclusion: 自旋-1强子的力学密度虽因Wigner旋转呈现更丰富的多极结构，但其物理内容完全由洛伦兹标量形式因子决定；Breit帧与IMF力学图像本质等价，仅表现形式不同，证实广义部分子分布与机械分布之间存在深层几何-动力学统一性。

Abstract: We construct an invertible map between the complete set of 3D Breit-frame pressure and shear force distributions and the constrained space of 2D infinite-momentum-frame pressure and shear force densities in coordinate space for spin-1 hadrons. The correspondence between the two sets of distributions in different frames is established by combining the geometric forward and inverse Abel transformations with recoil operators after separating the Wigner rotation induced mixing effects on the infinite-momentum-frame side. Unlike in the spin-1/2 case, these mixings complicate the coordinate space relations for spin-1 hadrons by introducing additional multipole structures, but the constraints among the form factors propagate into constraints on mechanical densities, so that these additional multipoles do not introduce new mechanical information.

</details>


### [108] [Electromagnetic form factors of vector mesons in Einstein-dilaton holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20736)
*Alfonso Ballon-Bayona,Tobias Frederico,Luis A. H. Mamani,Adão S. da Silva Junior,Wayne de Paula*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在基于Einstein-dilaton引力的全息QCD框架下，研究ρ介子族的电磁形状因子，采用谱分解与KK展开两种方法计算弹性及跃迁形状因子，并建立电流矩阵元的全息字典；结果与格点QCD弹性数据一致，跃迁形状因子为模型新预言；发现电形状因子零点、近似选择定则，并导出高动量转移下的渐近归一化与新超收敛求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解矢量介子（特别是ρ介子族）的内部电磁结构及其强相互作用动力学，弥补格点QCD在跃迁形状因子计算上的空白，并检验全息QCD在非微扰能区的可靠性。

Method: 基于Einstein-dilaton引力的全息QCD模型，采用confined背景度规；通过三点等位旋流关联函数的谱分解与Kaluza-Klein模展开双重方法计算电磁形状因子；构建电流矩阵元的全息字典以实现边界-体对偶映射。

Result: ρ介子族电形状因子在q² = 6 m_{ρⁿ}²处出现零点（基态为3.61 GeV²）；获得弹性与跃迁电磁形状因子；导出低q²下的无量纲电半径、磁矩与四极矩；高q²下满足标度律并给出渐近归一化；推导出含质量、衰变常数与耦合常数的新超收敛求和规则；发现矢量介子间强耦合的近似选择定则。

Conclusion: 该全息模型成功复现格点QCD弹性结果，为尚未计算的跃迁形状因子提供可靠预言；零点位置与光前/DS方程一致，验证了全息方法对非微扰QCD结构的描述能力；高q²结果支持超收敛性并拓展了QCD求和规则体系。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic form factors of the $ρ$ meson family within a holographic QCD framework based on Einstein-dilaton gravity with confining backgrounds. We obtain the elastic and transition form factors using a spectral decomposition of the three-point isospin current correlator and an independent approach based on the Kaluza-Klein expansion. Furthermore, we establish a holographic dictionary of the current matrix element. The elastic form factors are in agreement with available lattice QCD results, whereas the transition form factors, not yet determined within lattice QCD, are predictions of the model. For the elastic case, the electric form factor for the $ρ$ meson family exhibits a zero at $q^{2} = 6\, m_{ρ^{n}}^{2}$ and in particular for the ground state $q^{2} = 3.61\,\text{GeV}^{2}$, consistent with light-front and Dyson-Schwinger calculations. We also compute strong couplings between vector meson states and find some approximate selection rules. In the elastic case and low-$q^{2}$ regime, we calculate dimensionless electric radius, magnetic and quadrupole moments and compare with other approaches. In the high-$q^{2}$ regime, the form factors follow the expected scaling behavior, and the model predicts their asymptotic normalization. The results in the high-$q^{2}$ regime lead to superconvergence relations and establish a new sum rule involving masses, decay constants and couplings.

</details>


### [109] [The axion mass in the post-inflationary scenario in a minimal scaling model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20801)
*José Correia,Mark Hindmarsh,Joanes Lizarraga,Asier Lopez-Eiguren,Kari Rummukainen,Jon Urrestilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模经典场论模拟，计算了后暴胀场景下暗物质轴子的质量，得到其值为17.46(84) μeV，对应Peccei-Quinn对称性破缺能标约3.27×10¹¹ GeV，并指出非最小模型（含Higgs凝聚或规范通量的弦）的轴子质量预计低于40 μeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定轴子作为冷暗物质候选者的质量是连接粒子物理与宇宙学观测的关键问题；后暴胀场景中弦-畴壁网络的动力学主导轴子产额，需高精度数值模拟以克服解析近似的局限性。

Method: 采用12288³网格的经典标量场论格点模拟，追踪Peccei-Quinn U(1)对称性破缺后弦网络的形成、QCD相变时畴壁生成、以及弦-畴壁联合湮灭过程；结合格点QCD给出的拓扑磁化率，归一化轴子数密度至Hubble质量匹配时刻（mₐ = H*）。

Result: 轴子暗物质质量为17.46(84) μeV，对应fₐ = 3.27(15)×10¹¹ GeV及微波谐振腔探测频率4.22(20) GHz；轴子数密度为9.48(23)fₐ²H*（共动归一化）。

Conclusion: 在最小模型中，轴子质量由弦-畴壁网络的非平衡动力学严格决定；非最小模型因额外自由度（如Higgs凝聚或规范通量）增强湮灭效率，将压低轴子质量至<40 μeV，对实验搜寻具有明确指导意义。

Abstract: We calculate the dark matter axion mass in the post-inflationary scenario in a minimal model with a set of classical field theory simulations on $12\,288^3$ grids. In the model, the Peccei-Quinn U(1) symmetry is broken by a complex scalar field at a temperature $f_{\rm a} \sim 10^{11}$ GeV, creating a string network. The strings become the boundaries of domain walls at the QCD transition, which draw the strings together and annihilate them. The decay of the strings and domain walls leaves behind axion radiation, with a comoving number density estimated to be $9.48(23)f_{\rm a}^2 H_*$, normalised to unit scale factor when the axion mass is equal to the Hubble rate $H_*$. Using the most commonly quoted lattice results for the topological susceptibility, the dark matter axion mass in this minimal model is $17.46(84)\,μ\text{eV}$. This corresponds to a symmetry-breaking scale of $3.27(15) \times 10^{11}\, \text{GeV}$, and a haloscope resonant frequency of $4.22(20)\,\text{GHz}$. We argue that field theory simulations of non-minimal models where the strings have a Higgs condensate or gauge flux, and are annihilated by domain walls, will give axion mass predictions below about $40\,μ\text{eV}$.

</details>
